                            + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
        }

        boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
                && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
                && bearish(currentDecision)
                && fiveMinute != null
                && bearish(fiveDecision)
                && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
        if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
            return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
                    "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
        }

        return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
                + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
                + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
    }

    public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
        SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
        SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);

        // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
        if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
                    "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
                            + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                            + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
                            + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
        }

        // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
        if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
                && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
                && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
                    "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
                            + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                            + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
                            + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
        }

        if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
                    "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
                            + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
        }

        if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
                    "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
        }

        return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
                "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
                        + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
                        + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                        + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
    }

    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
        return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
    }

    private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
                || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
                || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
    }

    private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
    }

    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
    }

    private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
    }

    public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
        public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
        }

        public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
        }

        public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
        }
    }
}


===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionManagementService.java =====
package com.crypto.position.service;

import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.position.domain.PositionAnalysis;
import com.crypto.position.domain.PositionRecommendation;
import com.crypto.position.dto.PositionAnalysisView;
import com.crypto.position.repository.PositionAnalysisRepository;
import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletManagedPosition;
import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
import com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletAutoExecutionService;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Position manager for positions already owned by the automatic wallet.
 *
 * It compares the immutable BUY thesis stored on WalletManagedPosition with the
 * latest TradeSignal. It does not recalculate indicators or change the market
 * decision. HOLD, REDUCE, TAKE_PROFIT and EXIT remain advisory-only in v1.
 * A hard STOP_LOSS may be applied to the wallet through WalletAutoExecutionService.
 */
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class PositionManagementService {

    private static final BigDecimal ZERO = BigDecimal.ZERO;

    private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
    private final PositionAnalysisRepository analysisRepository;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final WalletAutoExecutionService walletAutoExecutionService;
    private final PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy priceAuthorityPolicy;
    private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;

    @Transactional
    public Optional<PositionAnalysis> analyze(TradeSignal signal) {
        if (!validSignal(signal)) return Optional.empty();

        String symbol = signal.getSymbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
                .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
                .orElse(null);
        if (!validPosition(position)) return Optional.empty();

        if (analysisRepository.existsByWalletPositionIdAndTradeSignalId(position.getId(), signal.getId())) {
            return analysisRepository.findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(position.getId());
        }

        BigDecimal entry = nvl(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
        BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
        BigDecimal pnl = price.subtract(entry).multiply(position.getQuantity());
        BigDecimal pnlPercent = percentageChange(entry, price);
        long holdingMinutes = Math.max(0,
                Duration.between(position.getOpenedAt(), Instant.now()).toMinutes());

        // A TradeSignal price can belong to a candle that closed BEFORE this position opened.
        // ALLOUSDT incident 2026-08-17: a delayed 5m signal carried 0.2806 from the
        // 18:15-18:19 candle and falsely stopped a position opened at 18:23 @ 0.2822.
        // Such signals are still valid thesis/MTF context, but they are forbidden from
        // driving price-based hard protection. Live TP/SL is handled by
        // LivePositionProtectionService from the actual live Binance price stream.
        boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
        PositionRecommendation hardRecommendation = authoritativePrice
                ? hardRiskRecommendation(position, price)
                : null;

        PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
                position.getEntryTrendScore(), position.getEntryStructureScore(),
                position.getEntryMomentumScore(), position.getEntryVolumeScore(),
                position.getEntryConfidence(), position.getEntryTotalScore(), signal);
        int trendDeterioration = thesis.trendPressure();
        int momentumExhaustion = thesis.momentumPressure();
        int profitProtection = profitProtection(pnlPercent, thesis);
        int riskEvents = authoritativePrice ? stopRiskPressure(position, price) : 0;
        int opportunityCost = 0; // intentionally excluded from v1
        int exitScore = Math.min(25, trendDeterioration + momentumExhaustion
                + profitProtection + riskEvents);

        int authority = timeframeAuthority(signal.getInterval());
        PositionRecommendation recommendation = hardRecommendation != null
                ? hardRecommendation
                : recommendation(exitScore, profitProtection, pnlPercent, authority);
        int confidence = confidence(exitScore, recommendation, hardRecommendation != null);

        List<String> evidence = evidence(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
                trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
                riskEvents, authority, hardRecommendation);
        String explanation = recommendation + " advisory: " + String.join("; ", evidence);

        Map<String, Object> details = details(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
                trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
                riskEvents, opportunityCost, exitScore, authority, evidence);

        PositionAnalysis saved = analysisRepository.save(PositionAnalysis.builder()
                .walletPosition(position)
                .tradeSignal(signal)
                .symbol(symbol)
                .intervalCode(signal.getInterval())
                .entryPriceUsdt(entry)
                .currentPriceUsdt(price)
                .unrealizedPnlUsdt(pnl)
                .unrealizedPnlPercent(pnlPercent)
                .holdingMinutes(holdingMinutes)
                .trendDeteriorationScore(trendDeterioration)
                .momentumExhaustionScore(momentumExhaustion)
                .profitProtectionScore(profitProtection)
                .riskEventScore(riskEvents)
                .opportunityCostScore(opportunityCost)
                .exitScore(exitScore)
                .recommendation(recommendation)
                .confidence(confidence)
                .explanation(limit(explanation, 2000))
                .detailsJson(toJson(details))
                .advisoryOnly(true)
                .analyzedAt(Instant.now())
                .build());

        if (recommendation == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {
            boolean applied = walletAutoExecutionService.executePositionStopLoss(saved);
            if (applied) {
                saved.setAdvisoryOnly(false);
                details.put("advisoryOnly", false);
                details.put("walletApplied", true);
                details.put("walletExecutionReason", "POSITION_STOP_LOSS");
                saved.setDetailsJson(toJson(details));
                saved = analysisRepository.save(saved);
                log.warn("Position STOP_LOSS applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
                        position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
            } else {
                log.error("Position STOP_LOSS was generated but not applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
                        position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
            }
        }

        log.info("Position analysis created: position={}, signal={}, symbol={}, recommendation={}, exitScore={}/25, advisoryOnly={}",
                position.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol, recommendation, exitScore, saved.isAdvisoryOnly());
        return Optional.of(saved);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PositionAnalysisView> latest() {
        return analysisRepository.findTop100ByOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc().stream()
                .map(this::view)
                .toList();
    }

    private boolean validSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
        return signal != null && signal.getId() != null && signal.getSymbol() != null
                && signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0;
    }

    private boolean validPosition(WalletManagedPosition position) {
        return position != null && position.getId() != null && position.getQuantity() != null
                && position.getQuantity().signum() > 0;
    }

    private PositionRecommendation hardRiskRecommendation(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
        if (p.getStopLossUsdt() != null && p.getStopLossUsdt().signum() > 0
                && price.compareTo(p.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
            return PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS;
        }
        if (p.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && p.getTakeProfitUsdt().signum() > 0
                && price.compareTo(p.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
            return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private int profitProtection(BigDecimal pnlPercent, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis) {
        if (pnlPercent.signum() <= 0) return 0;
        int score = 0;
        if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(5)) >= 0) score += 2;
        else if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(2)) >= 0) score += 1;
        if (thesis.trendDrop() >= 6 || thesis.structureDrop() >= 3) score += 2;
        if (thesis.momentumDrop() >= 5 || thesis.totalDrop() >= 15) score += 1;
        return Math.min(5, score);
    }

    private int stopRiskPressure(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
        BigDecimal stop = p.getStopLossUsdt();
        BigDecimal entry = p.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
        if (stop == null || stop.signum() <= 0 || entry == null || entry.compareTo(stop) <= 0) return 0;
        BigDecimal totalRoom = entry.subtract(stop);
        BigDecimal remaining = price.subtract(stop);
        if (remaining.signum() <= 0) return 4;
        BigDecimal ratio = remaining.divide(totalRoom, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.25)) <= 0) return 3;
        if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50)) <= 0) return 1;
        return 0;
    }

    private PositionRecommendation recommendation(int exitScore, int profitProtection,
                                                  BigDecimal pnlPercent, int authority) {
        // A 1m advisory can warn or recommend reduction, but cannot independently
        // invalidate the full position thesis.
        if (authority >= 2 && exitScore >= 17) return PositionRecommendation.EXIT;
        if (authority >= 2 && pnlPercent.signum() > 0
                && profitProtection >= 4 && exitScore >= 10) {
            return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
        }
        if (exitScore >= 10) return PositionRecommendation.REDUCE;
        return PositionRecommendation.HOLD;
    }

    private int timeframeAuthority(String interval) {
        if (interval == null) return 1;
        return switch (interval.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "1h", "4h", "1d" -> 3;
            case "5m", "15m", "30m" -> 2;
            default -> 1;
        };
    }

    private int confidence(int score, PositionRecommendation recommendation, boolean hardTrigger) {
        if (hardTrigger) return 95;
        int base = switch (recommendation) {
            case HOLD -> 85 - Math.min(30, score * 3);
            case REDUCE -> 65 + Math.min(20, Math.abs(score - 13) * 3);
            case TAKE_PROFIT -> 75 + Math.min(15, score);
            case STOP_LOSS -> 95;
            case EXIT -> 75 + Math.min(20, score - 16);
        };
        return Math.max(50, Math.min(95, base));
    }

    private List<String> evidence(WalletManagedPosition p, TradeSignal s, BigDecimal pnlPct,
                                  PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure t, int trend, int momentum, int protection,
                                  int risk, int authority, PositionRecommendation hard) {
        List<String> e = new ArrayList<>();
        e.add("Unrealized P/L is " + pnlPct.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "%");
        if (hard == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {
            e.add("Current price reached the immutable position stop loss " + p.getStopLossUsdt());
            return e;
        }
        if (hard == PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT) {
            e.add("Current price reached the immutable position take-profit target " + p.getTakeProfitUsdt());
            return e;
        }
        e.add("Trend changed " + t.entryTrend() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentTrend()
                + " and structure " + t.entryStructure() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentStructure());
        e.add("Momentum changed " + t.entryMomentum() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentMomentum()
                + " and volume " + t.entryVolume() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentVolume());
        e.add("Total score changed " + t.entryTotal() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentTotal()
                + "; confidence " + t.entryConfidence() + "ƒ+'" + t.currentConfidence());
        e.add("Thesis pressure: trend " + trend + "/8, momentum " + momentum + "/5");
        if (protection > 0) e.add("Profit-protection pressure " + protection + "/5");
        if (risk > 0) e.add("Price is approaching the stored stop-loss; risk pressure " + risk + "/4");
        e.add("Timeframe authority " + authority + "/3 for " + s.getInterval());
        if (trend <= 2 && momentum <= 1) e.add("Original BUY thesis remains broadly intact");
        return e;
    }

    private Map<String, Object> details(WalletManagedPosition p, TradeSignal s,
                                        BigDecimal pnlPct, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure t,
                                        int trend, int momentum, int protection,
                                        int risk, int opportunity, int exitScore,
                                        int authority, List<String> evidence) {
        Map<String, Object> details = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        details.put("advisoryOnly", true);
        details.put("method", "IMMUTABLE_BUY_THESIS_COMPARISON");
        details.put("entrySignalId", p.getEntrySignalId());
        details.put("entryDecision", p.getEntryDecision());
        details.put("entryPriceUsdt", p.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
        details.put("stopLossUsdt", p.getStopLossUsdt());
        details.put("takeProfitUsdt", p.getTakeProfitUsdt());
        details.put("currentPriceUsdt", s.getLatestPrice());
        details.put("unrealizedPnlPercent", pnlPct);
        details.put("timeframeAuthority", authority);
        details.put("entry", Map.of(
                "trend", t.entryTrend(), "structure", t.entryStructure(),
                "momentum", t.entryMomentum(), "volume", t.entryVolume(),
                "confidence", t.entryConfidence(), "total", t.entryTotal()));
        details.put("current", Map.of(
                "trend", t.currentTrend(), "structure", t.currentStructure(),
                "momentum", t.currentMomentum(), "volume", t.currentVolume(),
                "confidence", t.currentConfidence(), "total", t.currentTotal(),
                "decision", String.valueOf(s.getDecision())));
        details.put("deltas", Map.of(
                "trendDrop", t.trendDrop(), "structureDrop", t.structureDrop(),
                "momentumDrop", t.momentumDrop(), "volumeDrop", t.volumeDrop(),
                "confidenceDrop", t.confidenceDrop(), "totalDrop", t.totalDrop()));
        details.put("scores", Map.of(
                "trendDeterioration", trend,
                "momentumExhaustion", momentum,
                "profitProtection", protection,
                "hardRisk", risk,
                "opportunityCost", opportunity,
                "exitScore", exitScore,
                "maximum", 25));
        details.put("evidence", evidence);
        return details;
    }

    private BigDecimal percentageChange(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal current) {
        if (entry == null || entry.signum() == 0) return ZERO;
        return current.subtract(entry)
                .divide(entry, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
                .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
    }

    private PositionAnalysisView view(PositionAnalysis a) {
        return new PositionAnalysisView(a.getId(), a.getWalletPosition().getId(),
                a.getTradeSignal().getId(), a.getSymbol(), a.getIntervalCode(),
                a.getEntryPriceUsdt(), a.getCurrentPriceUsdt(), a.getUnrealizedPnlUsdt(),
                a.getUnrealizedPnlPercent(), a.getHoldingMinutes(), a.getExitScore(),
                a.getRecommendation(), a.getConfidence(), a.getExplanation(),
                a.isAdvisoryOnly(), a.getAnalyzedAt());
    }

    private String toJson(Object value) {
        try {
            return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(value);
        } catch (JsonProcessingException ex) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Could not serialize position analysis", ex);
        }
    }

    private BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value) {
        return value == null ? ZERO : value;
    }

    private String limit(String value, int max) {
        return value.length() <= max ? value : value.substring(0, max);
    }


}

 412:                 .openedAt(Instant.now())
 413:                 .build());

 415:     }

 417:     /** Kept for compatibility with older callers. */
 418:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
 419:     public Optional<PaperPosition> openFromSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 420:         return processSignal(signal);
 421:     }
 422: 
 423:     @Transactional
 424:     public PaperPosition close(Long positionId, BigDecimal exitPrice) {
 425:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.findById(positionId)
 426:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Position not found"));
 427: 
 428:         if (position.getStatus() != PositionStatus.OPEN) {
 429:             throw new IllegalStateException("Position is already closed");
 430:         }
 431: 
 432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
 433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
 435:     }
 436: 
 437:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 438:     public List<PaperPosition> list() {
 439:         return positionRepository.findTop100ByOrderByOpenedAtDesc();
 440:     }
 441: 
 442:     private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
 443:             PaperPosition position,
 444:             TradeSignal signal,
 445:             PositionStatus status,
 446:             String closeReason,
 447:             String explanation
 448:     ) {
 449:         return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
 450:     }
 451: 
 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
 537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 539:         }
 540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 541:     }
 542: 
 543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
 544:         Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
 545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
 546:         BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 547:                 .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
 548:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 549:                 .negate();
 550: 
 551:         if (pnl.compareTo(maximumLoss) <= 0) {
 552:             throw new IllegalStateException("Daily loss limit reached");
 553:         }
 554:     }
 555: }

  25: /**
  26:  * Mechanical protection for an already-open position using live Binance prices.
  27:  * TP / SL / Profit Lock must not wait for the next analysis signal.

  29: @Service

  50:     // FIX-153: direct observations use atomic effects+outcome; shared callers retain their ledger API.
  51:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired private com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService priceEvents;
  52:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  53:     @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
  54:     public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
  55:         price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
  56:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  57:         // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
  58:         try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
  59:         catch(RuntimeException failure){
  60:             // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
  61:             if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
  62:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  63:             throw failure;
  64:         }
  65:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();
  66:         String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
  67:         if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
  68:         if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
  69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
  70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
  71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
  72:     }

  74:     @Transactional
  75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {
  76:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return;
  77:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  78: 
  79:         // FIX-124: classify ONLY the first lock failure. The caller may retry after
  80:         // rollback in a fresh transaction; failures after evaluation are not retried.
  81:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  82:         WalletManagedPosition managed;
  83:         try {
  84:             managed = managedPositionRepository
  85:                     .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  86:                     .orElse(null);
  87:         } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
  88:             if (com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure)) {
  89:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  90:             }
  91:             throw failure;
  92:         }
  93:         if (managed == null || managed.getQuantity() == null || managed.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
  94: 
  95:         TradeSignal one = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1m").orElse(null);
  96:         TradeSignal five = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "5m").orElse(null);
  97:         TradeSignal hour = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1h").orElse(null);
  98: 
  99:         // Reaching TP is a management checkpoint, not an unconditional full exit.
 100:         if (managed.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 101:             // Pass the complete immutable BUY thesis. PositionContinuationPolicy and
 102:             // PositionManagementService now evaluate the same deterioration pressure (FIX-011).
 103:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(one, five, hour,
 104:                     managed.getEntryTrendScore(), managed.getEntryStructureScore(),
 105:                     managed.getEntryMomentumScore(), managed.getEntryVolumeScore(),
 106:                     managed.getEntryConfidence(), managed.getEntryTotalScore());
 107:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 108:                 BigDecimal distance = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt().subtract(managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
 109:                 BigDecimal oldTarget = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 110:                 BigDecimal newTarget = oldTarget.add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 111:                 Instant changedAt = Instant.now();
 112: 
 113:                 // FIX-118 behavioral phase: if Profit Lock was already earned under the old TP
 114:                 // but the wider approved target moves current progress back below activation,
 115:                 // persist TP_EXTENSION_REBASE before saving the new geometry. The historical
 116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
 117:                 // earned again.
 118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
 119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
 120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
 121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",
 123:                         managed.getId(), symbol, price, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 125:                         fix118Transition.previousState(), fix118Transition.state(), fix118Transition.progressPercent(),
 126:                         fix118Transition.activationPercent());
 127: 
 128:                 managed.setTakeProfitUsdt(newTarget);
 129:                 // FIX-11T: TP extension changes the planned entry->TP distance. Any old Near-TP
 130:                 // arm/rejection was relative to the superseded target and must be restarted.
 131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);
 132:                 managed.setUpdatedAt(changedAt);
 133:                 managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 134: 
 135:                 // FIX-067: Production has two persisted views of the same open lifecycle:
 136:                 // wallet_managed_position drives live protection, while paper_position is also
 137:                 // evaluated when a fresh TradeSignal arrives.  Before this fix only the managed
 138:                 // position received a TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED update.  The next 1m signal could
 139:                 // therefore see the stale ORIGINAL paper TP and close the wallet early.
 140:                 // PEPE wallet #833 / managed position #552 proved the race: managed TP was
 141:                 // extended 0.000004146897 -> 0.000004165346, but the signal path sold at
 142:                 // 0.000004150 because paper_position still held 0.000004146897.
 143:                 // Keep both Production state holders synchronized inside this same transaction.
 144:                 PaperPosition synchronizedPaper = paperPositionRepository
 145:                         .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN)
 146:                         .orElse(null);
 147:                 if (synchronizedPaper != null) {
 148:                     synchronizedPaper.setTakeProfit(newTarget);
 149:                     paperPositionRepository.save(synchronizedPaper);
 150:                 }
 151: 
 152:                 // FIX-053: Persist every TP revision so Dashboard can show the actual
 153:                 // Production management path instead of only the latest target value.
 154:                 positionManagementEventRepository.save(PositionManagementEvent.builder()
 155:                         .walletPositionId(managed.getId())
 156:                         .symbol(symbol)
 157:                         .eventType("TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED")
 158:                         .oldValueUsdt(oldTarget)
 159:                         .newValueUsdt(newTarget)
 160:                         .marketPriceUsdt(price)
 161:                         .reason(continuation.explanation())
 162:                         .occurredAt(changedAt)
 163:                         .build());
 164:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT extended: symbol={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, reason={}", symbol, oldTarget, newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 165:             } else if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 166:                     symbol, price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation())) {
 167:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), one);
 168:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT executed: symbol={}, price={}, target={}", symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 169:             }
 170:             return;
 171:         }
 172: 
 173:         // Stop loss is absolute and is never softened by continuation logic.
 174:         if (managed.getStopLossUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 175:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 176:                     symbol, price, "STOP_LOSS",
 177:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + managed.getStopLossUsdt())) {
 178:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS",
 179:                         "Live market price reached the configured stop loss.", one);
 180:                 log.info("Live STOP_LOSS executed: symbol={}, price={}, stop={}", symbol, price, managed.getStopLossUsdt());
 181:             }
 182:             return;
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation lock = dynamicProfitLockService.evaluatePrice(symbol, price);
 186: 
 187:         // FIX-118: during TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the superseded lock is intentionally
 188:         // non-executable, but earned profit is not left completely unprotected. Stop Loss
 189:         // has already had absolute priority above; this standalone floor is the next safety
 190:         // authority and does not depend on the old lock being triggered.
 191:         if (lock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 192:             String reason = lock.explanation();
 193:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}, target={}",
 194:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.hardProfitFloor(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 195:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 196:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         if (lock.active() || lock.triggered() || lock.rebasing()) {
 202:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_PATH] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, target={}, highest={}, active={}, " +
 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
 208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
 212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
 213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 215:                 }
 216:                 return;
 217:             }
 218: 
 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
 271:                 || !sameDecimal(managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), next.bestPrice())
 272:                 || managed.getNearTpBearishStreak() != next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute()
 273:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 274:         if (stateChanged) {
 275:             managed.setNearTpState(next.nearTpState());
 276:             managed.setNearTpBestPrice(next.bestPrice());
 277:             managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute());
 278:             managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 279:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 280:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 281:         }
 282: 
 283:         if (evaluation.transition() || stateChanged) {
 284:             log.info("FIX-11T Near-TP evaluation: positionId={}, symbol={}, state={}, code={}, price={}, bestPrice={}, " +
 285:                             "tpProgressPct={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, oneMinuteId={}, oneMinuteOriginal={}, " +
 286:                             "fiveMinuteId={}, fiveMinuteOriginal={}, detail={}",
 287:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), next.nearTpState(), evaluation.code(), price, next.bestPrice(),
 288:                     evaluation.tpProgressPercent(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(),
 289:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getId(),
 290:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 291:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getId(),
 292:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 293:                     evaluation.explanation());
 294:         } else if (log.isDebugEnabled() && evaluation.code().startsWith("HOLD_")) {
 295:             log.debug("FIX-11T Near-TP hold: positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, price={}, detail={}",
 296:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 297:         }
 298: 
 299:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return;
 300: 
 301:         WalletAutoExecutionService.PartialHarvestResult harvest = walletAutoExecutionService
 302:                 .executeNearTpPartialHarvest(managed.getSymbol(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 303:         if (!harvest.executed()) {
 304:             log.warn("FIX-11T Near-TP harvest not executed: positionId={}, symbol={}, status={}, price={}",
 305:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.status(), price);
 306:             // If execution did not happen, return to rejection monitoring rather than leaving
 307:             // the state stranded in FAILURE_CONFIRMED. Idempotency still lives in wallet_trade.
 308:             managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED);
 309:             if ("BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM".equals(harvest.status())
 310:                     || "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE".equals(harvest.status())) {
 311:                 // The skipped order consumed the current confirmation pair. Requiring fresh
 312:                 // bearish evidence prevents a retry on every subsequent market-price tick.
 313:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 314:             }
 315:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 316:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 317:             return;
 318:         }
 319: 
 320:         // Keep paper_position quantity/P&L synchronized with the managed wallet lifecycle.
 321:         // Without this, a later terminal signal close would calculate P&L on pre-harvest quantity.
 322:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(managed.getSymbol(), PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 323:         if (paper != null) {
 324:             paper.setQuantity(harvest.remainingQuantity());
 325:             BigDecimal priorRealized = paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl();
 326:             paper.setRealizedPnl(priorRealized.add(harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), MC));
 327:             paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 328:         }
 329: 
 330:         log.info("FIX-11T NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, " +
 331:                         "price={}, realizedPnl={}, realizedPnlPct={}",
 332:                 managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.soldQuantity(), harvest.remainingQuantity(),
 333:                 price, harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), harvest.realizedPnlPercent());
 334:     }
 335: 
 336:     private TradeSignal latestEligibleSignal(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal newest, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 337:         if (newest != null && newest.getGeneratedAt() != null && !newest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) {
 338:             return newest;
 339:         }
 340:         return tradeSignalRepository
 341:                 .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, evaluatedAt)
 342:                 .orElse(null);
 343:     }
 344: 
 345:     /**
 346:      * FIX-117: TP extension must not erase Near-TP protection that was already earned.
 347:      *
 348:      * Only ARMED and REJECTION_DETECTED change from the pre-FIX-117 behavior. INACTIVE
 349:      * still resets against the new geometry. FAILURE_CONFIRMED also keeps the existing
 350:      * reset behavior because that persisted/transient lifecycle has a real interruption
 351:      * window between state save and harvest resolution and is intentionally out of scope.
 352:      * PARTIAL_HARVESTED remains terminal through the existing harvest-used guard.
 353:      *
 354:      * plannedDistance is recalculated from the current TP by NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy
 355:      * on every evaluation, so the historical best price remains a valid market observation
 356:      * and does not need synthetic rebasing after an extension.
 357:      */
 358:     private void resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(WalletManagedPosition managed) {
 359:         if (managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) return;
 360: 
 361:         NearTpState currentState = managed.getNearTpState() == null
 362:                 ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState();
 363:         switch (currentState) {
 364:             case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
 365:                 // Existing behavior: no earned protection is carried into the new geometry.
 366:                 managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.INACTIVE);
 367:                 managed.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
 368:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 369:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 370:             }
 371:             case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> {
 372:                 // FIX-117: preserve earned protection and the real historical best price.
 373:             }
 374:             case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
 375:                 // Preserve earned protection/best price, but discard bearish persistence
 376:                 // accumulated under the old, tighter geometry. The next evaluation will
 377:                 // recalculate giveback against the new TP and either recover or remain rejected.
 378:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 379:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 380:             }
 381:             case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> {
 382:                 // Existing terminal behavior; normally returned above by nearTpHarvestUsed.
 383:             }
 384:         }
 385:     }
 386: 
 387:     private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
 388:         return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
 389:     }
 390: 
 391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
 392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
 393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 394:         if (paper == null) return;
 395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
 396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
 397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
 398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
 399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 402:         paper.setStatus(status);
 403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
 405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 407: 
 408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
 409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
 410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
 411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 412:     }

 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
 537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 539:         }
 540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 541:     }
 542: 
 543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
 544:         Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
 545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
 546:         BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 547:                 .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
 548:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 549:                 .negate();
 550: 
 551:         if (pnl.compareTo(maximumLoss) <= 0) {
 552:             throw new IllegalStateException("Daily loss limit reached");
 553:         }
 554:     }

 171:                 .executionPriceObservedAt(executionPriceObservedAt)
 172:                 .grossAmountUsdt(spend)
 173:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 174:                 .netAmountUsdt(spend)
 175:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 176:                 .executionReason(entryStage == null || entryStage.isBlank() ? "ENTRY_BUY" : entryStage)
 177:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 178:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 179:                 .notes("Automatic wallet BUY using " + normalizedPositionPercent + "% of the configured BUY budget")
 180:                 .executionMessage("BUY decision from trade signal #" + signal.getId()
 181:                         + " (decisionPrice=" + signal.getLatestPrice() + ") applied to wallet for " + pair
 182:                         + " at executionPrice=" + price + " and " + normalizedPositionPercent
 183:                         + "% size. " + (executionExplanation == null ? "" : executionExplanation))
 184:                 .build());
 185: 
 186:         if (newPosition) {
 187:             daily.setExecutedBuys(daily.getExecutedBuys() + 1);
 188:         }
 189:         daily.setEndingUsdt(usdt.getQuantity());
 190:         daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 191:         daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 192:         dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 193:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 194:         return true;
 195:     }
 196: 
 197:     @Transactional
 198:     public void executeSell(TradeSignal signal) {
 199:         executeSignalLinkedExit(signal, "SIGNAL_SELL",
 200:                 signal == null || signal.getId() == null ? "Signal SELL"
 201:                         : "SELL decision from trade signal #" + signal.getId() + " applied to wallet for " + normalizePair(signal.getSymbol()));
 202:     }
 203: 
 204:     /**
 205:      * FIX-028: execute the same signal-linked wallet liquidation while preserving the
 206:      * position engine's actual terminal reason. This method intentionally keeps the same
 207:      * idempotency key (signalId:SELL), quantities, balances and close mechanics as the
 208:      * legacy executeSell path; only audit metadata is corrected.
 209:      */
 210:     @Transactional
 211:     public void executeSignalLinkedExit(TradeSignal signal, String executionReason, String executionMessage) {
 212:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) return;
 213:         String key = signal.getId() + ":SELL";
 214:         String pair = normalizePair(signal.getSymbol());
 215:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 216:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN").orElse(null);
 217:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 218:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 219:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 220:         if (executionExists(key)) return;
 221: 
 222:         if (position == null || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
 223: 
 224:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 225:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 226:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 227:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 228:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return;
 229: 
 230:         BigDecimal price = positive(signal.getLatestPrice());
 231:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 232:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 233:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 234:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 235:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 236: 
 237:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 238:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 239:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 240:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 360:                         + " generated STOP_LOSS and sold " + quantity + " " + assetSymbol
 361:                         + " at " + price + " USDT")
 362:                 .build());
 363: 
 364:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
 365:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
 366:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
 367:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 368:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 369:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 370:                 });
 371:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 372:         return true;
 373:     }

 376:     /**
 377:      * Applies a Dynamic Profit Lock exit generated by the Position Manager.
 378:      * The source signal is used only for traceability; it does not need to be a SELL signal.
 379:      */
 380:     @Transactional
 381:     public boolean executeProfitLock(TradeSignal sourceSignal, BigDecimal executionPrice, BigDecimal protectedPrice) {
 382:         if (sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getId() == null || executionPrice == null || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
 383:             return false;
 384:         }
 385: 
 386:         String pair = normalizePair(sourceSignal.getSymbol());
 387:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 388:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
 389:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
 390:                 .orElse(null);
 391:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 392:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 393:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 394: 
 395:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
 396:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
 397:             return false;
 398:         }
 399: 
 400:         String key = "PROFIT_LOCK:" + position.getId();
 401:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
 402: 
 403:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 404:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 405:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 406:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 407:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
 408: 
 409:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
 410:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 411:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 412:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 413:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 414:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 415:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 416: 
 417:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 418:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 419:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 420:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 421:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 422:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 423:         }
 424:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
 425:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
 426:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
 427: 
 428:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
 429:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
 430:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
 431:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
 432:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 433:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
 434: 
 435:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
 436:                 .signal(sourceSignal)
 437:                 .executionKey(key)
 438:                 .symbol(pair)
 439:                 .side("SELL")
 440:                 .quantity(quantity)
 441:                 .priceUsdt(price)
 442:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
 443:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 444:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
 445:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
 446:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
 447:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
 448:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 449:                 .executionReason("POSITION_PROFIT_LOCK")
 450:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 451:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 452:                 .notes("Dynamic Profit Lock applied to wallet")
 453:                 .executionMessage("Dynamic Profit Lock for trade signal #" + sourceSignal.getId()
 454:                         + " sold " + quantity + " " + assetSymbol + " at " + price
 455:                         + " USDT; protected level was " + protectedPrice + " USDT")
 456:                 .build());
 457: 
 458:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
 459:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
 460:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
 461:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 462:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 463:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 464:                 });
 465:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 466:         return true;
 467:     }

 496:                 ? "MECHANICAL_EXIT"

 499:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;

 505:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
 506:

 508:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 509:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 510:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 511:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 512:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 513:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 514:

 516:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 517:         assetRepository.save(coin);

 519:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 520:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 521:         }

 539:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
 540:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 541:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
 542:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
 543:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
 544:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
 545:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 546:                 .executionReason(reason)
 547:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 548:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 549:                 .notes("Mechanical live-price position exit")
 550:                 .executionMessage(executionMessage == null ? reason + " triggered at " + price : executionMessage)
