  52:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  53:     @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
  54:     public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
  55:         price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
  56:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  57:         // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
  58:         try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
  59:         catch(RuntimeException failure){
  60:             // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
  61:             if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
  62:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  63:             throw failure;
  64:         }
  65:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();
  66:         String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
  67:         if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
  68:         if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
  69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
  70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
  71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     @Transactional
  75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {
  76:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return;
  77:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  78: 
  79:         // FIX-124: classify ONLY the first lock failure. The caller may retry after
  80:         // rollback in a fresh transaction; failures after evaluation are not retried.
  81:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  82:         WalletManagedPosition managed;
  83:         try {
  84:             managed = managedPositionRepository
  85:                     .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  86:                     .orElse(null);
  87:         } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
  88:             if (com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure)) {
  89:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  90:             }
  91:             throw failure;
  92:         }
  93:         if (managed == null || managed.getQuantity() == null || managed.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
  94: 
  95:         TradeSignal one = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1m").orElse(null);
  96:         TradeSignal five = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "5m").orElse(null);
  97:         TradeSignal hour = tradeSignalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, "1h").orElse(null);
  98: 
  99:         // Reaching TP is a management checkpoint, not an unconditional full exit.
 100:         if (managed.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 101:             // Pass the complete immutable BUY thesis. PositionContinuationPolicy and
 102:             // PositionManagementService now evaluate the same deterioration pressure (FIX-011).
 103:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(one, five, hour,
 104:                     managed.getEntryTrendScore(), managed.getEntryStructureScore(),
 105:                     managed.getEntryMomentumScore(), managed.getEntryVolumeScore(),
 106:                     managed.getEntryConfidence(), managed.getEntryTotalScore());
 107:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 108:                 BigDecimal distance = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt().subtract(managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
 109:                 BigDecimal oldTarget = managed.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 110:                 BigDecimal newTarget = oldTarget.add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 111:                 Instant changedAt = Instant.now();
 112: 
 113:                 // FIX-118 behavioral phase: if Profit Lock was already earned under the old TP
 114:                 // but the wider approved target moves current progress back below activation,
 115:                 // persist TP_EXTENSION_REBASE before saving the new geometry. The historical
 116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
 117:                 // earned again.
 118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
 119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
 120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
 121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",
 123:                         managed.getId(), symbol, price, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 125:                         fix118Transition.previousState(), fix118Transition.state(), fix118Transition.progressPercent(),
 126:                         fix118Transition.activationPercent());
 127: 
 128:                 managed.setTakeProfitUsdt(newTarget);
 129:                 // FIX-11T: TP extension changes the planned entry->TP distance. Any old Near-TP
 130:                 // arm/rejection was relative to the superseded target and must be restarted.
 131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);
 132:                 managed.setUpdatedAt(changedAt);
 133:                 managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 134: 
 135:                 // FIX-067: Production has two persisted views of the same open lifecycle:
 136:                 // wallet_managed_position drives live protection, while paper_position is also
 137:                 // evaluated when a fresh TradeSignal arrives.  Before this fix only the managed
 138:                 // position received a TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED update.  The next 1m signal could
 139:                 // therefore see the stale ORIGINAL paper TP and close the wallet early.
 140:                 // PEPE wallet #833 / managed position #552 proved the race: managed TP was
 141:                 // extended 0.000004146897 -> 0.000004165346, but the signal path sold at
 142:                 // 0.000004150 because paper_position still held 0.000004146897.
 143:                 // Keep both Production state holders synchronized inside this same transaction.
 144:                 PaperPosition synchronizedPaper = paperPositionRepository
 145:                         .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN)
 146:                         .orElse(null);
 147:                 if (synchronizedPaper != null) {
 148:                     synchronizedPaper.setTakeProfit(newTarget);
 149:                     paperPositionRepository.save(synchronizedPaper);
 150:                 }
 151: 
 152:                 // FIX-053: Persist every TP revision so Dashboard can show the actual
 153:                 // Production management path instead of only the latest target value.
 154:                 positionManagementEventRepository.save(PositionManagementEvent.builder()
 155:                         .walletPositionId(managed.getId())
 156:                         .symbol(symbol)
 157:                         .eventType("TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED")
 158:                         .oldValueUsdt(oldTarget)
 159:                         .newValueUsdt(newTarget)
 160:                         .marketPriceUsdt(price)
 161:                         .reason(continuation.explanation())
 162:                         .occurredAt(changedAt)
 163:                         .build());
 164:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT extended: symbol={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, reason={}", symbol, oldTarget, newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 165:             } else if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 166:                     symbol, price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation())) {
 167:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), one);
 168:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT executed: symbol={}, price={}, target={}", symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 169:             }
 170:             return;
 171:         }
 172: 
 173:         // Stop loss is absolute and is never softened by continuation logic.
 174:         if (managed.getStopLossUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 175:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 176:                     symbol, price, "STOP_LOSS",
 177:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + managed.getStopLossUsdt())) {
 178:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS",
 179:                         "Live market price reached the configured stop loss.", one);
 180:                 log.info("Live STOP_LOSS executed: symbol={}, price={}, stop={}", symbol, price, managed.getStopLossUsdt());
 181:             }
 182:             return;
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation lock = dynamicProfitLockService.evaluatePrice(symbol, price);
 186: 
 187:         // FIX-118: during TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the superseded lock is intentionally
 188:         // non-executable, but earned profit is not left completely unprotected. Stop Loss
 189:         // has already had absolute priority above; this standalone floor is the next safety
 190:         // authority and does not depend on the old lock being triggered.
 191:         if (lock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 192:             String reason = lock.explanation();
 193:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}, target={}",
 194:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.hardProfitFloor(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 195:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 196:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         if (lock.active() || lock.triggered() || lock.rebasing()) {
 202:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_PATH] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, target={}, highest={}, active={}, " +
 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
 208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
 212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
 213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 215:                 }
 216:                 return;
 217:             }
 218: 
 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }

 207:     /** FIX-138: emitted only after the transactional service proxy returned.
 208:      * Signal persistence is distinct from wallet processing and delivery completion. */
 209:     private static void logSaved(Long eventId,CandleClosedEvent event,TradeSignal signal,String mode) {
 210:         if(signal==null || signal.getId()==null) {
 211:             log.warn("[FIX-138][SIGNAL_SAVE_UNCONFIRMED] mode={}, event={}, symbol={}, interval={}; service returned no persisted signal identity",
 212:                 mode,eventId,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode());
 213:             return;
 214:         }
 215:         java.time.Instant returned=java.time.Instant.now();
 216:         Long liveDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getGeneratedAt()!=null
 217:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt()).toMillis():null;
 218:         Long insertionDelayMs="LIVE".equals(mode)&&event.closeTime()!=null&&signal.getCreatedAt()!=null
 219:             ?java.time.Duration.between(event.closeTime(),signal.getCreatedAt()).toMillis():null;
 220:         log.info("[FIX-153][SIGNAL_SAVED] mode={}, event={}, signal={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenUtc={}, candleCloseUtc={}, generatedUtc={}, rowCreatedUtc={}, serviceReturnedUtc={}, serviceReturnedKsa={}, liveCloseToGenerationMs={}, liveCloseToInsertionMs={}; rowCreatedUtc is row creation time, not database commit time; historical generatedUtc is an as-of clock, signal save does not imply a wallet trade",
 221:             mode,eventId,signal.getId(),event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),event.closeTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),signal.getCreatedAt(),returned,returned.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),liveDelayMs,insertionDelayMs);

 223:     private String localProcessingOutcome(Long signalId){
 224:         if(signalId==null)return "PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 225:         var statuses=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForList("SELECT status FROM signal_processing_work WHERE signal_id=?",String.class,signalId);
 226:         if(statuses.size()!=1)return "PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 227:         return switch(statuses.getFirst()){
 228:             case "COMPLETED"->"COMPLETED";
 229:             case "EXPIRED"->"EXECUTION_EXPIRED";
 230:             default->"PROCESSING_UNCONFIRMED";
 231:         };
 232:     }
 233:     /** FIX-140: Optional.empty is not proof of processing completion. */
 234:     private String processingOutcome(long event) {
 235:         var states=sharedWorkEvidence.queryForList("SELECT status FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=?",event);
 236:         if(states.size()!=1)return "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 237:         return switch(String.valueOf(states.getFirst().get("status"))) {
 238:             case "COMPLETED"->"COMPLETED";
 239:             case "EXPIRED"->"EXECUTION_EXPIRED";
 240:             case "LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED"->"EXECUTION_NOT_EXECUTED";
 241:             case "SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY"->"EXECUTION_RETRY";
 242:             default->"REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 243:         };
 244:     }

 246:     private static void traceStage(Long id, CandleClosedEvent event, String stage, long started) {
 247:         log.info("[FIX-138][WORKER_STAGE] event={}, symbol={}, interval={}, open={}, stage={}, elapsedMs={}",
 248:                 id,event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),stage,
 249:                 (System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000);
 250:     }
 251:     private String terminal(CandleClosedEvent event,String outcome,String reason,String workerOutcome){
 252:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),outcome,reason,null,"CANDLE_WORKER");
 253:         return workerOutcome;
 254:     }

  12: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;

  19: /** FIX-127: one coordinator for all Production signal callers. No retry of the
  20:  * existing body after it has begun; only the new initial lock is retry-marked. */
  21: @Service
  22: public class SignalProcessingCoordinator {
  23:     // FIX-125 mandatory in Spring; null only in existing constructor-only policy tests.
  24:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  25:     private com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletTransactionCoordination walletCoordination;
  26: 
  27:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(SignalProcessingCoordinator.class);
  28:     private final SignalProcessingStore store;
  29:     private final TradeSignalRepository signals;
  30:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positions;
  31:     private final SignalProcessingFreshness freshness;
  32:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  33:     public SignalProcessingCoordinator(SignalProcessingStore store, TradeSignalRepository signals,
  34:             WalletManagedPositionRepository positions, SignalProcessingFreshness freshness, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  35:         this.store=store;this.signals=signals;this.positions=positions;this.freshness=freshness;
  36:         transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  37:         transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  38:     }
  39:     public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
  40:             Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business) {
  41:         return process(signalId,background,business,null);
  42:     }
  43:     public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
  44:             Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business, Long sourceEventId) {
  45:         if(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  46:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 caller must be outside the processing transaction");
  47:         if(!background && !store.exists(signalId)) {
  48:             // Automatic callers have already registered atomically with creation.
  49:             // Explicit existing-signal calls register only at the user's request.
  50:             transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->store.register(signals.findById(signalId)
  51:                     .orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal not found: "+signalId)),ProcessingOrigin.EXPLICIT));
  52:         }
  53:         store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
  54:         for(int localAttempt=0;localAttempt<2;localAttempt++) {
  55:             var work=store.claim(signalId,background);
  56:             if(work==null) {
  57:                 log.info("[FIX-127][NOT_CLAIMED] signalId={}, background={}; no processing performed",signalId,background);
  58:                 return Optional.empty();
  59:             }
  60:             log.info("[FIX-127][ATTEMPT] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}, origin={}, attempt={}, background={}",
  61:                     signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.candleOpenTime(),work.origin(),work.attempts(),background);
  62:             java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger completion=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(-1);
  63:             try {
  64:                 Optional<PaperPosition> result=transaction.execute(tx->{
  65:                     org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(
  66:                         new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization() {
  67:                             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status) { completion.set(status); }
  68:                         });
  69:                     // FIX-140: observe the real transaction completion; a thrown exception alone
  70:                     // does not prove rollback. The private marker is only created here.
  71:                     if(walletCoordination!=null) {
  72:                         try { walletCoordination.symbol(work.symbol()); }
  73:                         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
  74:                             if(sourceEventId!=null && retryableSymbolLock(failure))throw new SymbolLockFailure(failure);
  75:                             throw failure;
  76:                         }
  77:                     }
  78:                     var owned=store.locked(signalId);
  79:                     if(owned==null || !"RUNNING".equals(owned.status()) || !work.owner().equals(owned.owner()))
  80:                         throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 processing ownership changed");
  81:                     // Acquire before PositionManagementService inserts a referencing
  82:                     // advisory or executes POSITION_STOP_LOSS. No rules evaluated yet.
  83:                     try {
  84:                         positions.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(work.symbol(),"OPEN");
  85:                     } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
  86:                         if(InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(ex))throw new InitialLockFailure(ex);
  87:                         throw ex;
  88:                     }
  89:                     store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
  90:                     // Read signal/freshness after lock acquisition: lock wait must not
  91:                     // consume the recovery grace before it is checked, or establish an
  92:                     // ordinary repeatable-read snapshot before the position is locked.
  93:                     TradeSignal signal=signals.findById(signalId).orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal missing"));
  94:                     if(!work.symbol().equals(signal.getSymbol()) || !work.interval().equals(signal.getInterval())
  95:                             || !work.candleOpenTime().equals(signal.getCandleOpenTime()))
  96:                         throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 signal lineage changed");
  97:                     // FIX-153: every claimed attempt, including the first direct
  98:                     // attempt regardless of origin, is checked after normal locks.
  99:                     if(!freshness.eligible(work))throw new Expired();
 100:                     Optional<PaperPosition> outcome=business.apply(signal);
 101:                     store.complete(work,outcome.map(PaperPosition::getId).orElse(null));
 102:                     return outcome;
 103:                 });
 104:                 log.info("[FIX-127][COMPLETED] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}; processing committed (not necessarily a trade)",
 105:                         signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts());
 106:                 return result;
 107:             } catch(Expired ex) {
 108:                 // Only exact RUNNING ownership can expire this attempt, after business TX rolled back.
 109:                 if(store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,"EXPIRED","FRESHNESS","Exact candle is no longer latest and fresh")!=1)
 110:                     throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 expiry ownership unconfirmed: signal="+signalId);
 111:                 log.warn("[FIX-127][EXPIRED] signalId={}, symbol={}, attempt={}",signalId,work.symbol(),work.attempts());
 112:                 return Optional.empty();
 113:             } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
 114:                 // FIX-140: only this invocation's initial symbol-lock marker plus a
 115:                 // confirmed rollback grants retry. Unknown connection/commit failures stay review-only.
 116:                 if(ex instanceof SymbolLockFailure && completion.get()==
 117:                         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_ROLLED_BACK) {
 118:                     String result=work.attempts()<4?"SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY":"LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED";
 119:                     int changed=store.finishSymbolLockFailure(work,result,ex.toString());
 120:                     if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-140 retry persistence unconfirmed",ex);
 121:                     log.warn("[FIX-140][SAFE_RETRY_RESULT] event={}, signal={}, attempt={}, outcome={}, rollback=CONFIRMED",
 122:                         sourceEventId,signalId,work.attempts(),result);
 123:                     if("SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY".equals(result))throw new SymbolLockRetryScheduled(signalId);
 124:                     return Optional.empty();
 125:                 }
 126:                 boolean initial=ex instanceof InitialLockFailure;
 127:                 String status=initial && work.attempts()<2 ? "RETRYABLE_FAILURE" : "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
 128:                 int recorded=store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,status,initial ? "INITIAL_POSITION_LOCK" : "PROCESSING_OR_COMMIT",ex.toString());
 129:                 log.error("[FIX-127][{}] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}, initialLock={}",
 130:                         recorded==1 ? status : "FAILURE_RECORD_NOT_UPDATED",signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts(),initial,ex);
 131:                 if(!initial || work.attempts()>=2)throw ex;
 132:                 // TransactionTemplate completed rollback before entering this catch.
 133:             }
 134:         }
 135:         return Optional.empty();
 136:     }
 137:     // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
 138:     // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
 139:     static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
 140:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 141:             if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;
 142:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 143:             if(e instanceof org.springframework.dao.QueryTimeoutException ||
 144:                e instanceof java.sql.SQLTimeoutException ||
 145:                e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && (sql.getErrorCode()==1205 || sql.getErrorCode()==1213))return true;
 146:         return false;
 147:     }
 148:     private static final class SymbolLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
 149:         SymbolLockFailure(Throwable cause) { super(cause); }
 150:     }
 151:     // A private marker cannot be supplied by a later business callback.
 152:     private static final class InitialLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
 153:         InitialLockFailure(RuntimeException cause) { super(cause); }
 154:     }
 155:     private static final class Expired extends RuntimeException {}
 156: }

 121:         // retain the historical-only marker used by non-executing catch-up rows.
 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");

 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));

 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: 
 198:     /**
 199:      * FIX-11M: Called only by RegressionTestWorker around the fresh-signal stage.
 200:      * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,
 201:      * ordering, thresholds, persistence, or any Production execution path.
 202:      */
 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 204:         replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
 205:     }

 207:     /**
 208:      * FIX-11M: Emit one aggregate Replay-only summary and always clear thread-local state.
 209:      */
 210:     public void finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 211:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 212:         replayAnalysisProfiler.remove();
 213:         if (profiler == null) {
 214:             log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_MISSING run={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false", runId);
 215:             return;
 216:         }
 217:         profiler.logSummary(log, runId);
 218:     }

  17: /**
  18:  * FIX-028: records the actual production exit cause without changing execution.
  19:  *
  20:  * Example that motivated the audit: BTC hit TAKE_PROFIT while the latest 1m signal
  21:  * was WATCH. The legacy wallet ledger called that SIGNAL_SELL because it reused the
  22:  * current signal as an execution carrier. This service preserves both facts:
  23:  * closeTrigger=TAKE_PROFIT and sourceSignalDecision=WATCH.
  24:  */
  25: @Service
  26: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  27: public class ProductionExitAuditService {
  28:     private final ProductionExitAuditRepository auditRepository;
  29:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
  30:     private final PositionAnalysisRepository positionAnalysisRepository;
  31: 
  32:     @Transactional
  33:     public void record(PaperPosition paper, TradeSignal sourceSignal, String closeTrigger, String explanation) {
  34:         if (paper == null || paper.getId() == null || paper.getExitPrice() == null || closeTrigger == null) return;
  35:         if (auditRepository.findTopByPaperPositionIdOrderByAuditedAtDesc(paper.getId()).isPresent()) return;
  36: 
  37:         WalletManagedPosition managed = paper.getSignal() == null ? null : managedPositionRepository
  38:                 .findTopByEntrySignalIdOrderByOpenedAtDesc(paper.getSignal().getId()).orElse(null);
  39:         PositionAnalysis analysis = managed == null || managed.getId() == null ? null : positionAnalysisRepository
  40:                 .findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(managed.getId()).orElse(null);
  41: 
  42:         auditRepository.save(ProductionExitAudit.builder()
  43:                 .paperPositionId(paper.getId())
  44:                 .walletPositionId(managed == null ? null : managed.getId())
  45:                 .symbol(paper.getSymbol())
  46:                 .closeTrigger(closeTrigger)
  47:                 .sourceSignalId(sourceSignal == null ? null : sourceSignal.getId())
  48:                 .sourceSignalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getDecision().name())
  49:                 .sourceSignalOriginalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision().name())
  50:                 .positionAnalysisId(analysis == null ? null : analysis.getId())
  51:                 .positionRecommendation(analysis == null || analysis.getRecommendation() == null ? null : analysis.getRecommendation().name())
  52:                 .entryPriceUsdt(paper.getEntryPrice())
  53:                 .exitPriceUsdt(paper.getExitPrice())
  54:                 .stopLossUsdt(paper.getStopLoss())
  55:                 .takeProfitUsdt(paper.getTakeProfit())
  56:                 .closeExplanation(explanation)
  57:                 .auditedAt(Instant.now())
  58:                 .build());
  59:     }

  43:     public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
  44:             Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business, Long sourceEventId) {
  45:         if(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  46:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 caller must be outside the processing transaction");
  47:         if(!background && !store.exists(signalId)) {
  48:             // Automatic callers have already registered atomically with creation.
  49:             // Explicit existing-signal calls register only at the user's request.
  50:             transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->store.register(signals.findById(signalId)
  51:                     .orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal not found: "+signalId)),ProcessingOrigin.EXPLICIT));
  52:         }
  53:         store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
  54:         for(int localAttempt=0;localAttempt<2;localAttempt++) {
  55:             var work=store.claim(signalId,background);
  56:             if(work==null) {
  57:                 log.info("[FIX-127][NOT_CLAIMED] signalId={}, background={}; no processing performed",signalId,background);
  58:                 return Optional.empty();
  59:             }
  60:             log.info("[FIX-127][ATTEMPT] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}, origin={}, attempt={}, background={}",
  61:                     signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.candleOpenTime(),work.origin(),work.attempts(),background);
  62:             java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger completion=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(-1);
  63:             try {
  64:                 Optional<PaperPosition> result=transaction.execute(tx->{
  65:                     org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(
  66:                         new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization() {
  67:                             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status) { completion.set(status); }
  68:                         });
  69:                     // FIX-140: observe the real transaction completion; a thrown exception alone
  70:                     // does not prove rollback. The private marker is only created here.
  71:                     if(walletCoordination!=null) {
  72:                         try { walletCoordination.symbol(work.symbol()); }
  73:                         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
  74:                             if(sourceEventId!=null && retryableSymbolLock(failure))throw new SymbolLockFailure(failure);
  75:                             throw failure;
  76:                         }
  77:                     }
  78:                     var owned=store.locked(signalId);
  79:                     if(owned==null || !"RUNNING".equals(owned.status()) || !work.owner().equals(owned.owner()))
  80:                         throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 processing ownership changed");
  81:                     // Acquire before PositionManagementService inserts a referencing
  82:                     // advisory or executes POSITION_STOP_LOSS. No rules evaluated yet.
  83:                     try {
  84:                         positions.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(work.symbol(),"OPEN");
  85:                     } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
  86:                         if(InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(ex))throw new InitialLockFailure(ex);
  87:                         throw ex;
  88:                     }
  89:                     store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
  90:                     // Read signal/freshness after lock acquisition: lock wait must not
  91:                     // consume the recovery grace before it is checked, or establish an
  92:                     // ordinary repeatable-read snapshot before the position is locked.
  93:                     TradeSignal signal=signals.findById(signalId).orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal missing"));
  94:                     if(!work.symbol().equals(signal.getSymbol()) || !work.interval().equals(signal.getInterval())
  95:                             || !work.candleOpenTime().equals(signal.getCandleOpenTime()))
  96:                         throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 signal lineage changed");
  97:                     // FIX-153: every claimed attempt, including the first direct
  98:                     // attempt regardless of origin, is checked after normal locks.
  99:                     if(!freshness.eligible(work))throw new Expired();

 101:                     store.complete(work,outcome.map(PaperPosition::getId).orElse(null));
 102:                     return outcome;
 103:                 });
 104:                 log.info("[FIX-127][COMPLETED] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}; processing committed (not necessarily a trade)",
 105:                         signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts());
 106:                 return result;
 107:             } catch(Expired ex) {
 108:                 // Only exact RUNNING ownership can expire this attempt, after business TX rolled back.
 109:                 if(store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,"EXPIRED","FRESHNESS","Exact candle is no longer latest and fresh")!=1)

 113:             } catch(RuntimeException ex) {

 115:                 // confirmed rollback grants retry. Unknown connection/commit failures stay review-only.

 132:                 // TransactionTemplate completed rollback before entering this catch.
 133:             }
 134:         }
 135:         return Optional.empty();
 136:     }
 137:     // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
 138:     // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
 139:     static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
 140:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 141:             if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;
 142:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
 143:             if(e instanceof org.springframework.dao.QueryTimeoutException ||
 144:                e instanceof java.sql.SQLTimeoutException ||
 145:                e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && (sql.getErrorCode()==1205 || sql.getErrorCode()==1213))return true;
 146:         return false;
 147:     }
 148:     private static final class SymbolLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
 149:         SymbolLockFailure(Throwable cause) { super(cause); }
 150:     }
 151:     // A private marker cannot be supplied by a later business callback.
 152:     private static final class InitialLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
 153:         InitialLockFailure(RuntimeException cause) { super(cause); }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());

 112:         return result;
 113:     }

 165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 168: 
 169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);

 180:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {

 183:         }

 187:         }
 188:

 190:                 "1m SELL confirmed by fresh bearish 5m context; 1h is not bullish.");

 110:     public TradeSignal analyzeForProcessing(TechnicalIndicator indicator,

 112:         TradeSignal signal = analyze(indicator);
 113:         processingStore.register(signal, origin,sourceEventId);
 114:         return signal;
 115:     }

 117:     @Transactional
 118:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecoveredForProcessing(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant at) {
 119:         TradeSignal signal = analyzeRecovered(indicator, at);
 120:         // This legacy LIVE-mode recovery route is execution-capable; it must not
 121:         // retain the historical-only marker used by non-executing catch-up rows.
 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");
 123:         signal = signalRepository.save(signal);
 124:         processingStore.register(signal, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.RECOVERY);
 125:         return signal;
 126:     }

 128:     /**
 129:      * Manual entry point used by controllers or recovery jobs.
 130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.
 131:      */
 132:     @Transactional
 133:     public TradeSignal analyze(String symbol, String interval) {
 134:         TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 135:                 .getLatest(symbol, interval)
 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));
 139: 
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: 
 198:     /**
 199:      * FIX-11M: Called only by RegressionTestWorker around the fresh-signal stage.
 200:      * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,
 201:      * ordering, thresholds, persistence, or any Production execution path.
 202:      */
 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 204:         replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
 205:     }
 206: 
 207:     /**
 208:      * FIX-11M: Emit one aggregate Replay-only summary and always clear thread-local state.
 209:      */
 210:     public void finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 211:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 212:         replayAnalysisProfiler.remove();
 213:         if (profiler == null) {
 214:             log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_MISSING run={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false", runId);
 215:             return;
 216:         }
 217:         profiler.logSummary(log, runId);
 218:     }

package com.crypto.domain;
public enum PositionStatus { OPEN, CLOSED, STOPPED, CANCELLED }

package com.crypto.audit.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * FIX-028 diagnostic/audit record for one completed production position.
 *
 * This entity deliberately separates the terminal close trigger (TAKE_PROFIT,
 * STOP_LOSS, genuine SELL signal, Profit Lock, etc.) from the latest market
 * signal used as context at that moment. It is read-only evidence for debugging;
 * it never participates in trading decisions or execution authority.
 */
@Entity
@Table(name = "production_exit_audit")
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class ProductionExitAudit {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "paper_position_id", unique = true)
    private Long paperPositionId;

    @Column(name = "wallet_position_id")
    private Long walletPositionId;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String symbol;

    @Column(name = "close_trigger", nullable = false, length = 40)
    private String closeTrigger;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_id")
    private Long sourceSignalId;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_decision", length = 30)
    private String sourceSignalDecision;

    @Column(name = "source_signal_original_decision", length = 30)
    private String sourceSignalOriginalDecision;

    @Column(name = "position_analysis_id")
    private Long positionAnalysisId;

    @Column(name = "position_recommendation", length = 20)
    private String positionRecommendation;

    @Column(name = "entry_price_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal entryPriceUsdt;

    @Column(name = "exit_price_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal exitPriceUsdt;

    @Column(name = "stop_loss_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal stopLossUsdt;

    @Column(name = "take_profit_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal takeProfitUsdt;

    @Column(name = "close_explanation", length = 2000)
    private String closeExplanation;

    @Column(name = "audited_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant auditedAt;
}

package com.crypto.wallet.domain;

import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.position.domain.PositionAnalysis;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

@Entity
@Table(name = "wallet_trade")
@Getter @Setter @Builder @NoArgsConstructor @AllArgsConstructor
public class WalletTrade {
    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "signal_id")
    private TradeSignal signal;
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "position_analysis_id")
    private PositionAnalysis positionAnalysis;
    @Column(name = "execution_key", length = 100)
    private String executionKey;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String symbol;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private String side;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal quantity;
    @Column(name = "price_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal priceUsdt;
    /** FIX-056 immutable signal-time price used for decision audit only. */
    @Column(name = "decision_price_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal decisionPriceUsdt;
    /** FIX-056 UTC timestamp of the canonical market event used as the fill authority. */
    @Column(name = "execution_price_observed_at")
    private Instant executionPriceObservedAt;
    @Column(name = "gross_amount_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal grossAmountUsdt;
    @Column(name = "fee_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal feeUsdt;
    @Column(name = "net_amount_usdt", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal netAmountUsdt;
    @Column(name = "cost_basis_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal costBasisUsdt;
    @Column(name = "realized_pnl_usdt", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnlUsdt;
    @Column(name = "realized_pnl_percent", precision = 20, scale = 8)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnlPercent;
    @Column(name = "execution_type", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String executionType;
    @Column(name = "execution_reason", length = 40)
    private String executionReason;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String status;
    @Column(name = "executed_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant executedAt;
    @Column(length = 500)
    private String notes;
    // FIX-126: complete execution explanations must not abort wallet persistence.
    @Column(name = "execution_message", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String executionMessage;
}

package com.crypto.position.service;

import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
 *
 * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
 * higher-timeframe breakdown:
 *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
 *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
 *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
 *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
 */
@Component
public class PositionExitPolicy {

    public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
        SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
        SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);

        boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
                && fiveMinute != null
                && !supportive(fiveDecision);
        if (oneHourBreakdown) {
            return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
                    "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
                            + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
                            + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
        }

        boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
                && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
                && bearish(currentDecision)
                && fiveMinute != null
                && bearish(fiveDecision)
                && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
        if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
            return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
                    "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
        }

        return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
                + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
                + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
    }

    public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
        SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
        SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
        SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);

        // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
        if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
                    "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
                            + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                            + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
                            + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
        }

        // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
        if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
                && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
                && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
                    "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
                            + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                            + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
                            + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
        }

        if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
                    "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
                            + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
        }

        if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
            return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
                    "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
        }

        return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
                "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
                        + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
                        + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
                        + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
    }

    private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
        return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
    }

    private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
                || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
                || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
    }

    private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
    }

    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
    }

    private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
        return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
    }

    public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
        public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
        }

        public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
        }

        public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
            return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
        }
    }
}

package com.crypto.position.service;

import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * Single authority for deciding whether a TradeSignal price is temporally valid for
 * price-based position protection (TP / SL / profit-lock / replay mark-to-market).
 *
 * IMPORTANT INCIDENT GUARD (ALLOUSDT 2026-08-17): a 5m signal generated at 18:23:28
 * carried the close of its 18:15-18:19 candle (0.2806). A position opened at 18:23:26
 * at 0.2822 was incorrectly stopped four seconds later because that historical candle
 * close was treated as a current market price. Signal prices remain valid as ANALYSIS
 * CONTEXT, but a candle whose market observation time predates the position may never
 * drive mechanical protection.
 */
@Service
public class PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy {

    public boolean canUseSignalPrice(TradeSignal signal, Instant positionOpenedAt) {
        if (signal == null || positionOpenedAt == null || signal.getLatestPrice() == null
                || signal.getLatestPrice().signum() <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
        Instant observedAt = marketObservationTime(signal);
        return observedAt != null && !observedAt.isBefore(positionOpenedAt);
    }

    /** Candle close/observation time, derived from candle open time + interval. */
    public Instant marketObservationTime(TradeSignal signal) {
        if (signal == null || signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null) return null;
        Duration interval = intervalDuration(signal.getInterval());
        return interval == null ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plus(interval);
    }

    private Duration intervalDuration(String interval) {
        if (interval == null) return null;
        return switch (interval.trim().toLowerCase()) {
            case "1m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(1);
            case "5m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
            case "15m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(15);
            case "1h" -> Duration.ofHours(1);
            case "4h" -> Duration.ofHours(4);
            case "1d" -> Duration.ofDays(1);
            default -> null;
        };
    }
}

package com.crypto.execution.service;

import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * FIX-056 fresh execution-price authority.
 *
 * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
 * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
 * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
 * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
 * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
 */
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
    private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);

    private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
    private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;

    public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
        // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
        // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
        // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
        Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
                "Execution price reference time is required");

        if (replayScope.active()) {
            return replayScope.latestPrice()
                    .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
                    .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
                    .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
                    .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
                    .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
        }

        return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
                .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
                .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
    }

    public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
}
